+# the public publishable/anon key needed by the browser client.

+REACT_APP_SUPABASE_KEY=your-supabase-publishable-or-anon-key
+
+# Cloudflare Pages Function runtime variables (set SUPABASE_ANON_KEY or
+# SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY, not both):
+# SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
+# SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-supabase-publishable-or-anon-key
+# The same public key is used by the Luna Function to verify Supabase sessions.
+# Never put a Supabase service-role key in any REACT_APP_* variable.

+# Cloudflare Pages and Supabase setup
+
+HerCycle uses Supabase Auth and RLS-protected tables directly from the browser. Cloudflare Pages Functions provide runtime Supabase configuration and the consent-gated Luna AI endpoint:
+
+- `GET /api/config` returns the Supabase project URL and **public** publishable/anon key to the same-origin app.
+- `POST /api/luna-chat` verifies the signed-in Supabase access token, then calls Workers AI only after explicit AI consent.
+
+## Cloudflare Pages variables and bindings
+
+Set these **Pages Function runtime variables** for both Production and Preview environments:
+
+- `SUPABASE_URL` — the Supabase project URL.
+- `SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` **or** `SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` — the Supabase publishable/anon key. This key is public by design and is subject to Supabase RLS.
+
+Never use a Supabase service-role key in HerCycle, in a `REACT_APP_*` variable, or in either public-key variable. `/api/config` returns only a recognized publishable/anon key; it rejects `sb_secret_*` values and service-role JWTs and does not return other variables.
+
+For Luna AI, also enable the Workers AI binding named exactly `AI`. `wrangler.toml` declares this binding for Wrangler deployments. Dashboard deployments must add the same binding in the Pages project's Functions settings. Optionally set `LUNA_MODEL` to a Workers AI model enabled for the account; otherwise Luna uses its fast default model.
+
+`/api/config` should return HTTP 200 with `configured: true` once the variables are set. `/api/luna-chat` requires a signed-in session, explicit AI consent, valid Supabase variables, and the `AI` binding; local Luna replies continue to work if AI is unavailable.
+
+## Build and Supabase Auth
+
+- Build command: `npm run build`
+- Output directory: `build`
+- Use Node.js 24 (`.nvmrc`; set `NODE_VERSION=24` if needed).
+- Keep the repository-root `functions/` directory in the Pages project so both `/api/*` Functions are deployed. `public/_routes.json` scopes Function execution to `/api/*`; `public/_redirects` deliberately avoids a catch-all rewrite so it cannot shadow the Function endpoints. Because this app has no `404.html`, Pages supplies the SPA fallback for client routes.
+- In Supabase Auth settings, enable Email auth and add your deployed Pages domain and any custom domain to the Site URL / allowed redirect URLs. HerCycle redirects email confirmation back to the current app origin.
+- For a new Supabase project, run `supabase/schema.sql` in the SQL Editor. For an existing project created from the earlier HerCycle schema, run `supabase/migrations/20261015000000_period_intimacy_metadata.sql`. It aligns the missing profile, energy, and period fields, adds the intimacy vibe, refreshes owner-only RLS/permissions, and updates the cycle RPC signature. It is safe to apply more than once; it has not been applied to a live project as part of this change.
+
+## Local development
+
+`npm start` runs Create React App, not Cloudflare Pages Functions. For a plain local preview, optional `.env.local` build variables can be used:
+
+- `REACT_APP_SUPABASE_URL`
+- `REACT_APP_SUPABASE_KEY` (publishable/anon key only)
+
+Without those local build variables, HerCycle remains usable in guest mode; account sync is enabled in the deployed Pages build through `/api/config`. To exercise Cloudflare Functions locally, use Wrangler Pages dev with the runtime variables and Workers AI binding configured.
+
+The intimacy share composer uses the device's share sheet, SMS composer, or clipboard after the user chooses to share. HerCycle does not send a partner message automatically or store the partner's contact/message; automated SMS/email delivery would require a separate messaging provider and its credentials.

+const jsonResponse = (body, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
+  status,
+  headers: {
+    'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
+    'Cache-Control': 'no-store, private',
+    'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
+  },
+})
+
+// Only return a recognized public Supabase key. This endpoint never reads a
+// service-role variable and rejects sb_secret_* keys and service-role JWTs.
+export async function onRequestGet({ env }) {
+  const supabaseUrl = String(env.SUPABASE_URL || '').trim().replace(/\/+$/, '')
+  const anonKey = String(env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY || '').trim()
+
+  if (!isValidSupabaseUrl(supabaseUrl) || !isPublicSupabaseKey(anonKey)) {
+    return jsonResponse({ configured: false }, 503)
+  }
+
+  return jsonResponse({
+    configured: true,
+    supabaseUrl,
+    anonKey,
+  })
+}
+
+const isValidSupabaseUrl = (value) => {
+  try {
+    const url = new URL(value)
+    const secure = url.protocol === 'https:'
+    const local = url.protocol === 'http:' && ['localhost', '127.0.0.1'].includes(url.hostname)
+    return (secure || local) && !url.username && !url.password && !url.search && !url.hash
+  } catch {
+    return false
+  }
+}
+
+const isPublicSupabaseKey = (value) => {
+  const key = String(value || '').trim()
+  if (/^sb_secret_/i.test(key)) return false
+  if (/^sb_publishable_[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/i.test(key)) return true
+
+  const jwtParts = key.split('.')
+  if (jwtParts.length !== 3) return false
+  try {
+    const base64 = jwtParts[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')
+    const decoded = atob(base64.padEnd(Math.ceil(base64.length / 4) * 4, '='))
+    return JSON.parse(decoded).role === 'anon'
+  } catch {
+    return false
+  }
+}

+const DEFAULT_MODEL = '@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct'
+const MAX_BODY_BYTES = 24000
+const MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 900
+const ALLOWED_PHASES = new Set(['menstrual', 'follicular', 'ovulatory', 'luteal', 'unknown'])
+
+const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are Luna, HerCycle’s AI wellness companion: warm, elegant, emotionally intelligent, feminine-coded in voice but inclusive in language, confident, reassuring, and lightly flirty or playful when the user welcomes it. You are an AI; do not claim human experiences or pretend to be the user's real-life partner.
+
+PERSONALITY AND CONVERSATION
+- Be body-positive, nonjudgmental, kind, and direct. Never shame, moralize, or assume gender, orientation, relationship status, sexual experience, or goals.
+- Match the user's tone: gentle when they are anxious, curious and clear when they ask for facts, warmly playful when they invite it. Don't turn every topic into flirtation or cycle talk.
+- Answer the latest question specifically and lead with the useful part. Use concise paragraphs or bullets; expand when asked. Define medical terms in accessible language.
+- Vary your openings, cadence, examples, and follow-ups. Do not fall back on stock lines like “Good question,” “I’m here for you,” or “Would you like the science, practical tips, or when to see a clinician?” in every reply. Don't add a question or praise when it doesn't help.
+- Read the recent conversation and recent-reply note. Never repeat a previous answer verbatim or recycle its distinctive phrasing. If the user asks again, offer a genuinely different angle or briefly ask what part still feels unanswered; don't loop through the same reassurance.
+- Be honest about uncertainty and limits. Don't invent sources, current facts, symptoms, personal details, or patterns.
+
+CYCLE AND REPRODUCTIVE HEALTH
+- Answer questions about periods, cycle phases and variability, common symptoms, ovulation, fertility awareness, PMS, PMDD, perimenopause, menopause, hormones, sleep, mood, energy, nutrition, exercise, and self-care.
+- Cycle timing varies; a 28-day cycle is not a rule. Tracking is an estimate, not a diagnosis, contraception, or guarantee of ovulation or pregnancy.
+- Use the supplied self-reported cycle summary only when relevant. Personalize from information actually present—such as the latest logged phase, period dates, or mood/energy ratings—and say when there is not enough data. Treat an average as descriptive, never predictive. Never infer libido, arousal, consent, or what the user wants from a cycle phase.
+- Never diagnose, promise fertility or pregnancy outcomes, prescribe medication or dosages, or advise stopping treatment. Recommend a qualified clinician or pharmacist for personal treatment, contraception, or testing decisions.
+- Severe or sudden pelvic pain, very heavy bleeding, fainting, fever with pelvic pain, possible pregnancy complications, or other emergency signs call for prompt medical care or local emergency services. Stay calm and specific.
+
+SEXUAL HEALTH, INTIMACY, AND RELATIONSHIPS
+- Welcome questions about desire, arousal, pleasure, anatomy, orgasm, lubrication, toys, contraception, STI prevention/testing, menstrual sex, pain during sex, communication, boundaries, and consent. Be sex-positive, accurate, inclusive, and practical; do not shame curiosity.
+- Consent must be freely given, specific, and changeable at any time. Never encourage pressure, coercion, or ignoring discomfort. Don't assume a partner's gender or that the user has a partner.
+- Keep sexual-health education factual and non-graphic. You may help write a classy, flirty, consent-forward message or a clearly fictional, non-explicit romantic scene, but do not generate explicit erotic roleplay or graphic sexual storytelling, and do not impersonate the user's real partner.
+- The product is intended for adults; do not ask users to prove their age. If a user explicitly says they are under 18, do not flirt or sexualize them; keep the conversation to factual, age-appropriate health and consent education.
+
+PRIVACY
+- Never request a name, email, account details, exact location, or other identifying information. Do not claim a conversation is anonymous or saved. The check-in summary is limited context, not permission to pry; intimacy logs and partner messages are not shared with you.`
+
+const jsonResponse = (body, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
+  status,
+  headers: {
+    'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
+    'Cache-Control': 'no-store, private',
+    'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
+  },
+})
+
+export async function onRequestPost({ request, env }) {
+  const origin = request.headers.get('Origin')
+  if (origin && origin !== new URL(request.url).origin) {
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'Cross-origin requests are not accepted.' }, 403)
+  }
+
+  if (!(request.headers.get('Content-Type') || '').includes('application/json')) {
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'Send a JSON request.' }, 415)
+  }
+
+  const declaredLength = Number(request.headers.get('Content-Length') || 0)
+  if (declaredLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'This message is too long.' }, 413)
+  }
+
+  let payload
+  try {
+    const rawBody = await request.text()
+    if (new TextEncoder().encode(rawBody).byteLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {
+      return jsonResponse({ error: 'This message is too long.' }, 413)
+    }
+    payload = JSON.parse(rawBody)
+  } catch {
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'The request could not be read.' }, 400)
+  }
+
+  if (payload?.aiConsent !== true) {
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'Enable AI replies in Luna before sending a message.' }, 403)
+  }
+
+  const messages = sanitizeMessages(payload?.messages)
+  if (!messages.length || messages[messages.length - 1].role !== 'user') {
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'Add a message before asking Luna.' }, 400)
+  }
+
+  const authHeader = request.headers.get('Authorization') || ''
+  const accessToken = authHeader.match(/^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i)?.[1]
+  const supabaseUrl = String(env.SUPABASE_URL || '').trim().replace(/\/+$/, '')
+  const supabaseKey = String(env.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY || '').trim()
+
+  if (!supabaseUrl || !isPublicSupabaseKey(supabaseKey)) {
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'Luna AI is not configured with a public Supabase key.' }, 503)
+  }
+  if (!accessToken) {
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'Sign in is required for personalized AI replies.' }, 401)
+  }
+
+  if (!env.AI || typeof env.AI.run !== 'function') {
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'Luna AI is not enabled for this deployment.' }, 503)
+  }
+
+  try {
+    const userResponse = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/auth/v1/user`, {
+      headers: {
+        apikey: supabaseKey,
+        Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
+      },
+    })
+
+    if (!userResponse.ok) {
+      return jsonResponse({ error: 'Your session could not be verified. Please sign in again.' }, 401)
+    }
+
+    // The verified user identity is deliberately not forwarded to the model.
+    const context = sanitizeCycleSummary(payload?.cycleSummary)
+    const contextMessage = context.checkInCount
+      ? `Optional self-reported cycle context (phase, mood, energy, the latest explicitly logged period start, and a retrospective average between distinct logged period starts when available; no name, email, account ID, symptom log, libido, or intimacy logs): ${JSON.stringify(context)}. Symptoms are not stored in this summary; only discuss symptoms the user mentions in the current conversation.`
+      : 'The user has no saved cycle check-ins available to you. Do not assume personal patterns or infer a period date, symptoms, or cycle length.'
+
+    const recentAssistantReplies = messages
+      .filter((item) => item.role === 'assistant')
+      .slice(-3)
+      .map((item) => item.content.slice(0, 600))
+    const recentReplyNote = recentAssistantReplies.length
+      ? `Recent Luna replies to avoid echoing: ${JSON.stringify(recentAssistantReplies)}`
+      : 'There are no earlier Luna replies to avoid repeating.'
+    const buildPromptMessages = (retry = false) => [
+      { role: 'system', content: SYSTEM_PROMPT },
+      { role: 'system', content: contextMessage },
+      {
+        role: 'system',
+        content: retry
+          ? `Your draft echoed a recent response. Try again with a genuinely fresh, specific answer to the latest user message. Do not reuse the opening, structure, or closing question. ${recentReplyNote}`
+          : `Vary this reply and avoid repeating prior wording. ${recentReplyNote}`,
+      },
+      ...messages,
+    ]
+    const model = env.LUNA_MODEL || DEFAULT_MODEL
+    const generationOptions = { max_tokens: 760, temperature: 0.82, top_p: 0.92 }
+    let result = await env.AI.run(model, { messages: buildPromptMessages(), ...generationOptions })
+    let reply = extractModelReply(result)
+
+    if (isRecentDuplicate(reply, recentAssistantReplies)) {
+      result = await env.AI.run(model, { messages: buildPromptMessages(true), ...generationOptions })
+      const retryReply = extractModelReply(result)
+      if (retryReply.trim()) reply = retryReply
+    }
+
+    if (typeof reply !== 'string' || !reply.trim()) {
+      return jsonResponse({ error: 'Luna could not create a reply just now.' }, 502)
+    }
+
+    return jsonResponse({ reply: reply.trim().slice(0, 4000), mode: 'ai' })
+  } catch (error) {
+    // Avoid logging prompt, messages, health context, or credentials.
+    console.error('Luna AI request failed:', error?.name || 'UnknownError')
+    return jsonResponse({ error: 'Luna is taking a quiet moment. Please try again.' }, 502)
+  }
+}
+
+function extractModelReply(result) {
+  return typeof result === 'string'
+    ? result
+    : result?.response || result?.result?.response || ''
+}
+
+function isRecentDuplicate(candidate, recentReplies) {
+  const normalizedCandidate = normalizeAssistantText(candidate)
+  return Boolean(normalizedCandidate && recentReplies.some((reply) => normalizeAssistantText(reply) === normalizedCandidate))
+}
+
+function normalizeAssistantText(value) {
+  return String(value || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/gu, ' ').trim()
+}
+
+const sanitizeMessages = (items) => {
+  if (!Array.isArray(items)) return []
+  return items
+    .slice(-12)
+    .filter((item) => item && (item.role === 'user' || item.role === 'assistant'))
+    .map((item) => ({
+      role: item.role,
+      content: typeof item.content === 'string' ? item.content.trim().slice(0, MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) : '',
+    }))
+    .filter((item) => item.content.length > 0)
+}
+
+const sanitizeCycleSummary = (input) => {
+  if (!input || typeof input !== 'object') return { checkInCount: 0 }
+  const count = clampInteger(input.checkInCount, 0, 10000)
+  const phaseCounts = {}
+
+  if (input.phaseCounts && typeof input.phaseCounts === 'object') {
+    for (const phase of ALLOWED_PHASES) {
+      const value = clampInteger(input.phaseCounts[phase], 0, 10000)
+      if (value > 0) phaseCounts[phase] = value
+    }
+  }
+
+  const recentCheckIns = Array.isArray(input.recentCheckIns)
+    ? input.recentCheckIns.slice(0, 8).map((item) => ({
+      daysAgo: clampInteger(item?.daysAgo, 0, 3650),
+      phase: ALLOWED_PHASES.has(item?.phase) ? item.phase : 'unknown',
+      mood: clampScale(item?.mood),
+      energy: clampScale(item?.energy),
+    }))
+    : []
+
+  return {
+    checkInCount: count,
+    averageMood: clampScale(input.averageMood),
+    averageEnergy: clampScale(input.averageEnergy),
+    phaseCounts,
+    mostCommonPhase: ALLOWED_PHASES.has(input.mostCommonPhase) ? input.mostCommonPhase : null,
+    lastPeriodStartDate: sanitizeDate(input.lastPeriodStartDate),
+    averageCycleLength: clampCycleLength(input.averageCycleLength),
+    cycleLengthObservations: clampInteger(input.cycleLengthObservations, 0, 100),
+    recentCheckIns,
+  }
+}
+
+const clampInteger = (value, min, max) => {
+  const number = Number(value)
+  return Number.isFinite(number) ? Math.min(max, Math.max(min, Math.floor(number))) : 0
+}
+
+const sanitizeDate = (value) => {
+  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) return null
+  const date = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00.000Z`)
+  return Number.isFinite(date.getTime()) && date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === value ? value : null
+}
+
+const clampCycleLength = (value) => {
+  const number = Number(value)
+  return Number.isFinite(number) && number >= 1 && number <= 120
+    ? Math.round(number * 10) / 10
+    : null
+}
+
+const clampScale = (value) => {
+  const number = Number(value)
+  return Number.isFinite(number) && number >= 1 && number <= 10
+    ? Math.round(number * 10) / 10
+    : null
+}
+
+const isPublicSupabaseKey = (value) => {
+  const key = String(value || '').trim()
+  if (/^sb_secret_/i.test(key)) return false
+  if (/^sb_publishable_[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/i.test(key)) return true
+
+  const jwtParts = key.split('.')
+  if (jwtParts.length !== 3) return false
+  try {
+    const base64 = jwtParts[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')
+    const decoded = atob(base64.padEnd(Math.ceil(base64.length / 4) * 4, '='))
+    return JSON.parse(decoded).role === 'anon'
+  } catch {
+    return false
+  }
+}

+import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
+import { NavLink, Outlet, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion'
+import {
+  FiActivity,
+  FiCalendar,
+  FiDroplet,
+  FiHeart,
+  FiHome,
+  FiLogIn,
+  FiLogOut,
+  FiMessageCircle,
+  FiSettings,
+} from 'react-icons/fi'
+import { GiLotus } from 'react-icons/gi'
+import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
+
+import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'
+import LunaWidget from './LunaWidget'
+
+const primaryNavigation = [
+  { to: '/', label: 'dashboard', icon: FiHome, end: true },
+  { to: '/log', label: 'cycleLog', icon: FiDroplet },
+  { to: '/insights', label: 'insights', icon: FiActivity },
+  { to: '/luna', label: 'luna', icon: FiMessageCircle },
+  { to: '/intimacy', label: 'intimacy', icon: FiHeart },
+]
+
+const routeTitles = {
+  '/': ['dashboard', 'dashboardDescription'],
+  '/log': ['cycleLog', 'checkInDescription'],
+  '/insights': ['insightsTitle', 'insightsDescription'],
+  '/luna': ['lunaTitle', 'lunaDescription'],
+  '/intimacy': ['intimacy', 'intimacyDescription'],
+  '/settings': ['settings', 'settingsDescription'],
+}
+
+const AppShell = ({ session }) => {
+  const { t } = useTranslation()
+  const location = useLocation()
+  const navigate = useNavigate()
+  const [hideIntimacy, setHideIntimacy] = useState(() => {
+    try {
+      return JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('hercycle.preferences') || '{}').hideIntimacy || false
+    } catch {
+      return false
+    }
+  })
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const syncPreferences = (event) => {
+      if (event.detail && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(event.detail, 'hideIntimacy')) {
+        setHideIntimacy(Boolean(event.detail.hideIntimacy))
+        return
+      }
+      try {
+        setHideIntimacy(Boolean(JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('hercycle.preferences') || '{}').hideIntimacy))
+      } catch {
+        setHideIntimacy(false)
+      }
+    }
+
+    window.addEventListener('hercycle:preferences-changed', syncPreferences)
+    return () => window.removeEventListener('hercycle:preferences-changed', syncPreferences)
+  }, [])
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!session?.user?.id) return undefined
+    let isMounted = true
+    supabase
+      .from('user_settings')
+      .select('hide_intimacy')
+      .eq('user_id', session.user.id)
+      .maybeSingle()
+      .then(({ data, error }) => {
+        if (isMounted && !error && data?.hide_intimacy !== undefined && data.hide_intimacy !== null) {
+          setHideIntimacy(data.hide_intimacy)
+        }
+      })
+      .catch((error) => console.error('Unable to load navigation preferences:', error))
+    return () => { isMounted = false }
+  }, [session?.user?.id])
+
+  const visiblePrimaryNavigation = hideIntimacy
+    ? primaryNavigation.filter((item) => item.to !== '/intimacy')
+    : primaryNavigation
+  const [titleKey, descriptionKey] = routeTitles[location.pathname] || routeTitles['/']
+  const userEmail = session?.user?.email
+  const userInitial = userEmail?.charAt(0)?.toUpperCase() || 'H'
+
+  const handleAccountAction = async () => {
+    if (!session) {
+      navigate('/auth')
+      return
+    }
+
+    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut()
+    if (error) {
+      console.error('Could not sign out:', error)
+      return
+    }
+    navigate('/auth')
+  }
+
+  const renderNavigationLink = ({ to, label, icon: Icon, end }) => (
+    <NavLink
+      key={to}
+      to={to}
+      end={end}
+      title={t(label)}
+      className={({ isActive }) => `app-nav-link${isActive ? ' is-active' : ''}`}
+    >
+      <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
+      <span>{t(label)}</span>
+    </NavLink>
+  )
+
+  return (
+    <div className="app-shell">
+      <div className="app-sparkle-field" aria-hidden="true"><span>✦</span><span>✧</span><span>✧</span></div>
+      <aside className="app-sidebar">
+        <NavLink to="/" className="app-brand" aria-label="HerCycle home">
+          <span className="app-brand-mark"><GiLotus aria-hidden="true" /></span>
+          <span className="app-brand-copy">
+            <strong>hercycle</strong>
+            <small>care in your rhythm</small>
+          </span>
+        </NavLink>
+
+        <div className="app-sidebar-label">YOUR SPACE</div>
+        <nav className="app-navigation" aria-label="Main navigation">
+          {visiblePrimaryNavigation.map(renderNavigationLink)}
+        </nav>
+
+        <div className="app-sidebar-note">
+          <span className="app-sidebar-note-icon"><GiLotus aria-hidden="true" /></span>
+          <p>A gentle reminder</p>
+          <strong>Your body is not a problem to solve.</strong>
+        </div>
+
+        <div className="app-sidebar-bottom">
+          {renderNavigationLink({ to: '/settings', label: 'settings', icon: FiSettings })}
+          <button type="button" className="app-nav-link app-account-action" title={session ? t('signOut') : t('signIn')} onClick={handleAccountAction}>
+            {session ? <FiLogOut aria-hidden="true" /> : <FiLogIn aria-hidden="true" />}
+            <span>{session ? t('signOut') : t('signIn')}</span>
+          </button>
+          <div className="app-profile-chip">
+            <span className="app-profile-avatar">{userInitial}</span>
+            <span className="app-profile-copy">
+              <strong>{userEmail ? userEmail.split('@')[0] : 'Your private space'}</strong>
+              <small>{session ? 'Synced securely' : 'Just for you'}</small>
+            </span>
+            <FiCalendar className="app-profile-flower" aria-hidden="true" />
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      </aside>
+
+      <div className="app-main-column">
+        <header className="app-topbar">
+          <div className="app-topbar-heading">
+            <span className="app-mobile-brand"><GiLotus aria-hidden="true" /> hercycle</span>
+            <span className="app-topbar-kicker">YOUR CYCLE COMPANION</span>
+            <h1>{t(titleKey)}</h1>
+            <p>{t(descriptionKey)}</p>
+          </div>
+          <div className="app-topbar-actions">
+            <span className="app-date-chip"><FiCalendar aria-hidden="true" />{new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }).format(new Date())}</span>
+            {!session && (
+              <button type="button" className="app-sign-in-button" onClick={() => navigate('/auth')}>
+                {t('signIn')}
+              </button>
+            )}
+          </div>
+        </header>
+
+        <main className="app-page-content">
+          <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
+            <motion.div
+              key={location.pathname}
+              className="app-route-transition"
+              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
+              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
+              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -6 }}
+              transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: 'easeOut' }}
+            >
+              <Outlet />
+            </motion.div>
+          </AnimatePresence>
+        </main>
+      </div>
+
+      <nav className="app-mobile-nav" aria-label="Main navigation" style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${visiblePrimaryNavigation.length + 1}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }}>
+        {visiblePrimaryNavigation.map(({ to, label, icon: Icon, end }) => (
+          <NavLink
+            key={to}
+            to={to}
+            end={end}
+            className={({ isActive }) => `app-mobile-nav-link${isActive ? ' is-active' : ''}`}
+          >
+            <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
+            <span>{t(label)}</span>
+          </NavLink>
+        ))}
+        <NavLink
+          to="/settings"
+          className={({ isActive }) => `app-mobile-nav-link${isActive ? ' is-active' : ''}`}
+        >
+          <FiSettings aria-hidden="true" />
+          <span>{t('settings')}</span>
+        </NavLink>
+      </nav>
+
+      <LunaWidget key={session?.user?.id || 'guest'} session={session} />
+    </div>
+  )
+}
+
+export default AppShell

diff --git a/src/components/LunaWidget.js b/src/components/LunaWidget.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..5b2576a573fff8fafd485afc241f8f43f9dd37b6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/LunaWidget.js
@@ -0,0 +1,350 @@
+import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
+import { Alert } from '@mui/material'
+import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion'
+import { FiArrowRight, FiArrowUpRight, FiHeart, FiLock, FiSend, FiShield, FiTrash2, FiX } from 'react-icons/fi'
+import { GiLotus } from 'react-icons/gi'
+import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
+import { useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
+
+import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'
+import {
+  MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH,
+  makeLocalReply,
+  readAiPreference,
+  readOnboardingPhase,
+  readOnboardingTone,
+  requestLunaReply,
+  summarizeCycles,
+  writeAiPreference,
+} from '../lib/luna'
+
+const LunaWidget = ({ session }) => {
+  const { t } = useTranslation()
+  const location = useLocation()
+  const navigate = useNavigate()
+  const userId = session?.user?.id || null
+  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false)
+  const [cycles, setCycles] = useState([])
+  const [isLoadingCycles, setIsLoadingCycles] = useState(Boolean(userId))
+  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
+  const [isThinking, setIsThinking] = useState(false)
+  const [aiPreference, setAiPreference] = useState(() => readAiPreference(userId))
+  const [connectionMode, setConnectionMode] = useState(null)
+  const [chatError, setChatError] = useState('')
+  const [messages, setMessages] = useState(() => [{
+    id: 'luna-widget-welcome',
+    role: 'assistant',
+    content: t('lunaHello'),
+  }])
+  const launcherRef = useRef(null)
+  const inputRef = useRef(null)
+  const messagesEndRef = useRef(null)
+  const activeRequestRef = useRef(null)
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let isMounted = true
+    setCycles([])
+    setAiPreference(readAiPreference(userId))
+    setIsLoadingCycles(Boolean(userId))
+    setChatError('')
+
+    if (!userId) {
+      setIsLoadingCycles(false)
+      return () => { isMounted = false }
+    }
+
+    supabase
+      .from('cycles')
+      .select('date, phase, mood, energy, period_start_date')
+      .eq('user_id', userId)
+      .order('date', { ascending: false })
+      .limit(60)
+      .then(({ data, error }) => {
+        if (!isMounted) return
+        if (error) {
+          console.error('Luna widget could not load cycle context:', error)
+          setChatError(t('lunaContextLoadError'))
+        } else {
+          setCycles(data || [])
+        }
+        setIsLoadingCycles(false)
+      })
+      .catch((error) => {
+        if (!isMounted) return
+        console.error('Luna widget could not load cycle context:', error)
+        setChatError(t('lunaContextLoadError'))
+        setIsLoadingCycles(false)
+      })
+
+    return () => { isMounted = false }
+  }, [userId, t])
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const syncConsent = (event) => {
+      if (!event.detail || event.detail.userId === userId) {
+        setAiPreference(event.detail?.preference || readAiPreference(userId))
+      }
+    }
+    window.addEventListener('hercycle:luna-consent-changed', syncConsent)
+    return () => window.removeEventListener('hercycle:luna-consent-changed', syncConsent)
+  }, [userId])
+
+  useEffect(() => () => activeRequestRef.current?.abort(), [])
+
+  const cycleSummary = useMemo(() => summarizeCycles(cycles), [cycles])
+  const aiConsent = aiPreference === 'yes'
+  const consentIsPending = Boolean(userId) && aiPreference === 'unset'
+  const canChat = !isThinking
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!isOpen) return undefined
+    const timeout = window.setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 90)
+    return () => window.clearTimeout(timeout)
+  }, [isOpen])
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!isOpen || !messagesEndRef.current) return
+    const reduceMotion = Boolean(window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches)
+    messagesEndRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduceMotion ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'end' })
+  }, [isOpen, messages, isThinking])
+
+  const allowAiChat = () => {
+    writeAiPreference(userId, 'yes')
+    setAiPreference('yes')
+  }
+
+  const continueLocally = () => {
+    writeAiPreference(userId, 'no')
+    setAiPreference('no')
+  }
+
+  const turnOffAiChat = () => {
+    activeRequestRef.current?.abort()
+    writeAiPreference(userId, 'no')
+    setAiPreference('no')
+    setConnectionMode('local')
+    setMessages([{ id: `luna-widget-welcome-${Date.now()}`, role: 'assistant', content: t('lunaHello') }])
+    setChatError('')
+  }
+
+  const clearConversation = () => {
+    activeRequestRef.current?.abort()
+    setMessage('')
+    setMessages([{ id: `luna-widget-welcome-${Date.now()}`, role: 'assistant', content: t('lunaHello') }])
+    setChatError('')
+    inputRef.current?.focus()
+  }
+
+  const sendMessage = async (suggestedMessage) => {
+    const text = String(suggestedMessage ?? message).trim().slice(0, MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH)
+    if (!text || !canChat) return
+
+    const userMessage = { id: `luna-widget-user-${Date.now()}`, role: 'user', content: text }
+    const nextMessages = [...messages, userMessage]
+    setMessages(nextMessages.slice(-40))
+    setMessage('')
+    setChatError('')
+    setIsThinking(true)
+
+    let controller = null
+    try {
+      let reply = null
+      if (session?.access_token && aiConsent) {
+        controller = new AbortController()
+        activeRequestRef.current = controller
+        reply = await requestLunaReply(session.access_token, nextMessages, cycleSummary, controller.signal)
+      }
+
+      if (reply) {
+        setConnectionMode('ai')
+        setMessages((current) => [...current, { id: `luna-widget-ai-${Date.now()}`, role: 'assistant', content: reply }].slice(-40))
+      } else {
+        setConnectionMode('local')
+        if (session?.access_token && aiConsent) setChatError(t('lunaAiUnavailable'))
+        setMessages((current) => [...current, {
+          id: `luna-widget-local-${Date.now()}`,
+          role: 'assistant',
+          content: makeLocalReply(text, cycleSummary, readOnboardingPhase(), readOnboardingTone(), nextMessages),
+        }].slice(-40))
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (error?.name === 'AbortError') return
+      console.info('Luna widget is using local replies because AI is unavailable.', error)
+      setConnectionMode('local')
+      if (session?.access_token && aiConsent) setChatError(t('lunaAiUnavailable'))
+      setMessages((current) => [...current, {
+        id: `luna-widget-local-${Date.now()}`,
+        role: 'assistant',
+        content: makeLocalReply(text, cycleSummary, readOnboardingPhase(), readOnboardingTone(), nextMessages),
+      }].slice(-40))
+    } finally {
+      if (activeRequestRef.current === controller) activeRequestRef.current = null
+      setIsThinking(false)
+    }
+  }
+
+  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
+    event.preventDefault()
+    sendMessage()
+  }
+
+  const closeWidget = () => {
+    setIsOpen(false)
+    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => launcherRef.current?.focus())
+  }
+
+  const handleKeyDown = (event) => {
+    if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey) {
+      event.preventDefault()
+      sendMessage()
+    }
+    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+      event.stopPropagation()
+      closeWidget()
+    }
+  }
+
+  const openFullChat = () => {
+    setIsOpen(false)
+    navigate('/luna')
+  }
+
+  if (location.pathname === '/luna') return null
+
+  const suggestions = [t('lunaPromptPatterns'), t('lunaPromptIntimacy'), t('lunaPromptGeneral')]
+  const statusLabel = connectionMode === 'ai' ? t('lunaModeAi') : connectionMode === 'local' ? t('lunaModeLocal') : aiConsent ? t('lunaModeAiReady') : t('lunaModeReady')
+
+  return (
+    <div className={`luna-widget-root${isOpen ? ' is-open' : ''}`}>
+      <AnimatePresence>
+        {isOpen && (
+          <motion.section
+            className="luna-widget-panel"
+            role="dialog"
+            id="luna-widget-dialog"
+            aria-modal="false"
+            aria-labelledby="luna-widget-title"
+            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 14, scale: 0.98 }}
+            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
+            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 10, scale: 0.98 }}
+            transition={{ duration: 0.2, ease: 'easeOut' }}
+            onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === 'Escape') closeWidget() }}
+          >
+            <header className="luna-widget-header">
+              <div className="luna-widget-avatar" aria-hidden="true">
+                <GiLotus />
+                <span>✦</span>
+              </div>
+              <div className="luna-widget-heading">
+                <h2 id="luna-widget-title">Luna</h2>
+                <span><i className={connectionMode === 'ai' ? 'is-ai' : ''} />{statusLabel}</span>
+              </div>
+              <div className="luna-widget-header-actions">
+                <button type="button" className="luna-widget-clear" onClick={clearConversation} title={t('lunaClearChat')} aria-label={t('lunaClearChat')}>
+                  <FiTrash2 aria-hidden="true" />
+                </button>
+                <button type="button" className="luna-widget-close" onClick={closeWidget} title={t('lunaWidgetClose')} aria-label={t('lunaWidgetClose')}>
+                  <FiX aria-hidden="true" />
+                </button>
+              </div>
+            </header>
+
+            {consentIsPending && (
+              <div className="luna-widget-consent">
+                <p className="luna-widget-consent-summary">{t('lunaPrivacySummary')}</p>
+                <details>
+                  <summary><FiShield aria-hidden="true" /> {t('lunaWidgetDetailsLabel')}</summary>
+                  <p>{t('lunaPrivacyConsent')}</p>
+                </details>

+                    {item.role === 'assistant' && <span className="luna-widget-speaker">Luna</span>}

+                      <div className="luna-widget-typing" role="status" aria-label={t('lunaThinking')}><i /><i /><i /></div>
+                    </div>
+                  </motion.div>
+                )}
+              </AnimatePresence>
+              <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
+            </div>
+

+              <div className="luna-widget-suggestions">
+                {suggestions.map((suggestion) => (
+                  <button key={suggestion} type="button" onClick={() => sendMessage(suggestion)} disabled={!canChat}>

+                ))}
+              </div>
+            )}

+                rows={1}
+                maxLength={MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}
+                placeholder={t('lunaWidgetPlaceholder')}

+                <FiSend aria-hidden="true" />
+              </button>
+            </form>

+      <motion.button
+        ref={launcherRef}
+        type="button"
+        className="luna-widget-launcher"
+        aria-expanded={isOpen}
+        aria-controls="luna-widget-dialog"

+      </motion.button>
+    </div>
+  )

+import { formatPeriodDate, localDateString } from './period'
+
+export const MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 900
+const phaseNames = ['menstrual', 'follicular', 'ovulatory', 'luteal']
+
+export const consentStorageKey = (userId) => `hercycle.luna.aiConsent.${userId || 'guest'}`
+
+export const readAiPreference = (userId) => {
+  try {
+    const preference = window.sessionStorage.getItem(consentStorageKey(userId))
+    return preference === 'yes' || preference === 'no' ? preference : 'unset'
+  } catch {
+    return 'unset'
+  }
+}
+
+export const writeAiPreference = (userId, preference) => {
+  try {
+    window.sessionStorage.setItem(consentStorageKey(userId), preference)
+  } catch {
+    // The current component can still respect the preference in memory.
+  }
+  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('hercycle:luna-consent-changed', {
+    detail: { userId: userId || null, preference },
+  }))
+}
+
+export const readOnboardingPhase = () => {
+  try {
+    return JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('hercycle.onboarding') || '{}').phase_selected || null
+  } catch {
+    return null
+  }
+}
+
+export const readOnboardingTone = () => {
+  try {
+    return JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('hercycle.onboarding') || '{}').tone_preference || 'balanced'
+  } catch {
+    return 'balanced'
+  }
+}
+
+export const requestLunaReply = async (accessToken, messages, cycleSummary, signal) => {
+  const response = await fetch('/api/luna-chat', {
+    method: 'POST',
+    credentials: 'same-origin',
+    cache: 'no-store',
+    signal,
+    headers: {
+      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+      Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
+    },
+    body: JSON.stringify({
+      aiConsent: true,
+      messages: messages.slice(-12).map(({ role, content }) => ({ role, content })),
+      cycleSummary,
+    }),
+  })
+
+  const contentType = response.headers.get('content-type') || ''
+  if (!response.ok || !contentType.includes('application/json')) {
+    throw new Error(`Luna AI service unavailable (${response.status}).`)
+  }
+  const result = await response.json()
+  if (typeof result.reply !== 'string' || !result.reply.trim()) {
+    throw new Error('Luna returned an empty response.')
+  }
+  return result.reply.trim().slice(0, 4000)
+}
+
+export const summarizeCycles = (records = []) => {
+  const sorted = [...records].sort((a, b) => String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)))
+  const phaseCounts = sorted.reduce((counts, entry) => {
+    if (phaseNames.includes(entry.phase)) counts[entry.phase] = (counts[entry.phase] || 0) + 1
+    return counts
+  }, {})
+  const mostCommonPhase = Object.entries(phaseCounts).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0] || null
+  const averageMood = averageField(sorted, 'mood')
+  const averageEnergy = averageField(sorted, 'energy')
+  const periodStartDates = [...new Set(sorted
+    .map((entry) => entry.period_start_date)
+    .filter((date) => isValidDateString(date) && date <= localDateString()))].sort()
+  const cycleLengths = periodStartDates.slice(1).map((date, index) =>
+    dateStringDistance(periodStartDates[index], date)
+  ).filter((days) => Number.isFinite(days) && days > 0)
+  const averageCycleLength = cycleLengths.length
+    ? Math.round((cycleLengths.reduce((sum, days) => sum + days, 0) / cycleLengths.length) * 10) / 10
+    : null
+  const lastPeriodStartDate = periodStartDates[periodStartDates.length - 1] || null
+  const today = localDateAtNoon(new Date())
+  const recentCheckIns = sorted.slice(0, 8).map((entry) => {
+    const checkInDate = localDateAtNoon(new Date(`${entry.date}T12:00:00`))
+    return {
+      daysAgo: Math.max(0, Math.round((today - checkInDate) / 86400000)),
+      phase: phaseNames.includes(entry.phase) ? entry.phase : 'unknown',
+      mood: numericScale(entry.mood),
+      energy: numericScale(entry.energy),
+    }
+  })
+
+  return {
+    checkInCount: sorted.length,
+    averageMood,
+    averageEnergy,
+    phaseCounts,
+    mostCommonPhase,
+    lastPeriodStartDate,
+    averageCycleLength,
+    cycleLengthObservations: cycleLengths.length,
+    recentCheckIns,
+  }
+}
+
+export const makeLocalReply = (message, summary = {}, currentPhase, tone = 'balanced', conversation = []) => {
+  const query = String(message || '').toLowerCase().trim()
+  const choose = (replies) => chooseLocalVariant(replies, conversation)
+  const checkInCount = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(summary.checkInCount) || 0))
+  const averageMood = numericScale(summary.averageMood)
+  const averageEnergy = numericScale(summary.averageEnergy)
+  const latestLoggedPhase = summary.recentCheckIns?.[0]?.phase
+  const phase = latestLoggedPhase && latestLoggedPhase !== 'unknown'
+    ? latestLoggedPhase
+    : currentPhase || summary.mostCommonPhase
+  const phaseLabel = phase ? phase.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + phase.slice(1) : null
+
+  if (/^(hi|hey|hello|hiya|good morning|good evening|what's up|whats up)[!.?\s]*$/.test(query)) {
+    const greetings = [
+      'Hello, lovely. I’m glad you’re here—what’s on your mind?',
+      'Well, hello. What would you like to talk through today?',
+      'Hey, sunshine. Come as you are; where shall we start?',
+      'Hi there. I’m listening—what’s happening in your world?',
+      'A pleasure to see you. What would feel helpful to chat about?',
+    ]
+    return choose(tone === 'playful' ? [greetings[2], greetings[3], greetings[0], greetings[1], greetings[4]] : greetings)
+  }
+
+  if (/^(thanks|thank you|thx|ty)[!.?\s]*$/.test(query)) {
+    return choose([
+      'You’re very welcome. I’m glad that helped.',
+      'Anytime. What else would you like to untangle?',
+      'Of course. Take what’s useful and leave the rest.',
+      'You’re welcome, lovely. I’m here if another question comes up.',
+    ])
+  }
+
+  if (/\bhow are you\b|\bhow's your day\b|\bhows your day\b/.test(query)) {
+    return choose([
+      'I don’t have a day of my own, but I’m here and ready to listen. How has yours been?',
+      'I’m right here, curious and all ears. What kind of day are you having?',
+      'No errands or coffee for me—just a little space for you. How are you feeling today?',
+      'I’m here with a calm corner and an open mind. How are you arriving?',
+    ])
+  }
+
+  if (/\b(joke|make me laugh|something funny)\b/.test(query)) {
+    return choose([
+      'Why did the period tracker bring a tiny umbrella? It heard the forecast might be a little cycle-y. I’ll see myself out.',
+      'I tried to write a joke about hormones, but the timing was all over the place.',
+      'What did one cycle phase say to the other? “Let’s not make this a regular thing.”',
+      'My favorite period pun? I had one, but it’s gone with the flow.',
+    ])
+  }
+
+  if (/\b(suicid|self.?harm|abuse|coerc|assault|unsafe at home)\b/.test(query)) {
+    return choose([
+      'I’m sorry you’re facing this. You deserve support and safety. If you’re in immediate danger, move to a safer place if you can and contact local emergency services or someone you trust. You don’t have to share details here.',
+      'That sounds serious, and you deserve care without judgment. If there’s immediate danger, please reach local emergency help or a trusted person now. I can help you think through one safe next step.',
+      'Your safety comes first. If someone may hurt you or you may hurt yourself, contact local emergency services or a trusted person who can stay with you. You can keep the details private here.',
+      'I’m glad you said something. Please reach out to a trusted person or local crisis/emergency support, especially if you’re in immediate danger. We can take this one step at a time.',
+    ])
+  }
+
+  if (/\b(severe|sudden|faint|fainting|very heavy|soaking|fever|possible pregnancy complication)\b/.test(query)
+    && /\b(pain|pelvic|bleed|bleeding|period|pregnan|fever|cramp)\b/.test(query)) {
+    return choose([
+      'Severe or sudden pelvic pain, fainting, very heavy bleeding, fever with pelvic pain, or possible pregnancy complications need prompt medical attention. Please contact local emergency services or urgent care now.',
+      'Those symptoms can need urgent assessment, especially severe pain, fainting, heavy bleeding, or fever with pelvic pain. Please seek urgent medical care rather than relying on a chat reply.',
+      'I can’t assess this safely in chat. If the pain is severe or sudden, you feel faint, bleeding is very heavy, or pregnancy complications are possible, please get urgent medical help now.',
+      'Please take those symptoms seriously. Contact a qualified clinician urgently or local emergency services if symptoms are severe, worsening, or you feel faint.',
+    ])
+  }
+
+  if (/pattern|notice|check.?in|trend|learn/.test(query)) {
+    if (checkInCount < 3) {
+      return choose([
+        `I can see ${checkInCount} saved ${checkInCount === 1 ? 'check-in' : 'check-ins'} so far, which isn’t enough to call a personal pattern. We can stick to what you’ve recorded and keep noticing over time.`,
+        `Your log has ${checkInCount} ${checkInCount === 1 ? 'entry' : 'entries'} right now. I don’t want to turn a small sample into a story about your body; a few more check-ins may give us a clearer picture.`,
+        `I’m only seeing ${checkInCount} saved ${checkInCount === 1 ? 'check-in' : 'check-ins'}, so I can describe those entries but not a trend yet. No need to track perfectly—just when it’s useful to you.`,
+        `There are ${checkInCount} saved ${checkInCount === 1 ? 'note' : 'notes'} to work with. That’s a start, not enough to infer a pattern, and certainly not a prediction.`,
+      ])
+    }
+
+    const facts = []
+    if (averageMood !== null) facts.push(`average mood ${averageMood.toFixed(1)}/10`)
+    if (averageEnergy !== null) facts.push(`average energy ${averageEnergy.toFixed(1)}/10`)
+    if (summary.mostCommonPhase) facts.push(`${summary.mostCommonPhase} is the phase logged most often`)
+    const snapshot = facts.length ? facts.join('; ') : 'you have logged check-ins across multiple days'
+    return choose([
+      `Here’s the factual snapshot from ${checkInCount} check-ins: ${snapshot}. It describes your entries, not a rule or a forecast.`,
+      `Looking only at what you saved, I see ${snapshot} across ${checkInCount} check-ins. That’s an observation—not a prediction about what you’ll feel next.`,
+      `Your logbook, not a rulebook: ${checkInCount} check-ins show ${snapshot}. What part of that feels most useful or surprising to you?`,
+      `From ${checkInCount} check-ins, the clearest summary is ${snapshot}. Bodies vary, so I’d treat this as a note to explore rather than a conclusion.`,
+    ])
+  }
+
+  if (/energy|tired|fatigue|sleep|exhaust/.test(query)) {
+    if (averageEnergy !== null) {
+      return choose([
+        `Your saved energy ratings average ${averageEnergy.toFixed(1)}/10. That number can’t explain the cause by itself; sleep, stress, illness, food, and lots of other things can matter.`,
+        `I can see an average energy rating of ${averageEnergy.toFixed(1)}/10 in your check-ins. It’s a snapshot, not a verdict. What has your energy felt like today?`,
+        `Your logged energy averages ${averageEnergy.toFixed(1)} out of 10. If you’re feeling depleted, we can look at what’s been different lately without assuming it’s your cycle.`,
+        `The entries you saved put average energy at ${averageEnergy.toFixed(1)}/10. Want to explore practical ways to make today gentler, or what might be affecting it?`,
+      ])
+    }
+    return choose([
+      'Energy shifts for many reasons, and I don’t have enough saved check-ins to compare your personal pattern. What has today been asking of you?',
+      'I can’t tell what’s behind your energy without more context, and your cycle is only one possible influence. Would a practical reset or a little cycle science help more?',
+      'Feeling tired can have many causes. I don’t have a saved energy trend to draw on, but we can start with what feels different today.',
+      'I don’t see enough energy check-ins to describe a trend. For right now, what’s one thing you could make a little easier on yourself?',
+    ])
+  }

+  if (/mood|sad|anxious|irritable|overwhelm|stress/.test(query)) {

+      return choose([
+        `Your saved mood ratings average ${averageMood.toFixed(1)}/10. That’s a snapshot of your entries, not a judgment about you or an explanation of why you feel this way.`,
+        `I can see an average mood rating of ${averageMood.toFixed(1)}/10 in your check-ins. A cycle can be one piece of the picture, but it can’t explain everything.`,
+        `Your logged mood averages ${averageMood.toFixed(1)} out of 10. If today feels heavy, we can talk through what’s happening without forcing it into a pattern.`,

+      'I’m sorry things feel tender. You don’t need to justify that feeling. Would you like to tell me what’s weighing on you, or would a small grounding idea help?',

+      'You’re allowed to feel exactly as you do. I can listen, help sort through what’s stressing you, or offer one small reset—your call.',
+      'I hear you. No judgment and no need to make a pattern out of it. What has been the hardest part of today?',
+    ])

+
+  if (/cycle length|length of (my )?cycle|average cycle|how long.{0,20}cycle/.test(query)) {
+    if (summary.averageCycleLength !== null && summary.averageCycleLength !== undefined) {
+      const intervalCount = Number(summary.cycleLengthObservations) || 1
+      return choose([
+        `Your saved period starts give ${intervalCount} interval${intervalCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}, averaging ${summary.averageCycleLength} days. That’s a retrospective average, not a prediction of your next cycle.`,
+        `From ${intervalCount} interval${intervalCount === 1 ? '' : 's'} between period-start dates you recorded, the average is ${summary.averageCycleLength} days. Bodies vary, so I’d treat it as a snapshot rather than a rule.`,
+        `The period-start dates in your log average ${summary.averageCycleLength} days apart across ${intervalCount} interval${intervalCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}. It describes past entries; it can’t guarantee what comes next.`,
+        `Looking only at explicit period starts you saved, I calculate about ${summary.averageCycleLength} days on average (${intervalCount} interval${intervalCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}). Would you like help understanding what cycle length means?`,
+      ])
+    }
+    return choose([
+      'I need at least two distinct period-start dates to calculate an interval, and I don’t see enough saved yet. I won’t guess from cycle phases.',
+      'I don’t have enough explicitly recorded period starts to work out your average cycle length. One or more dates would let me describe past intervals, not predict the next one.',
+      'There aren’t two separate period-start dates in the saved context I can see, so I can’t calculate an average yet. I’d rather be accurate than fill in a number.',
+      'I can explain cycle length generally, but I don’t see enough period-start dates to calculate your personal average. The estimate needs multiple starts.',
+    ])
+  }
+
+  if (/last period|last menstrual|when did (my )?(last )?period|period start date/.test(query)) {
+    if (summary.lastPeriodStartDate) {
+      const dateLabel = formatPeriodDate(summary.lastPeriodStartDate, { weekday: undefined, year: 'numeric' })
+      return choose([
+        `The latest period start date you explicitly recorded is ${dateLabel}. That’s the date in your log, not a prediction.`,
+        `Your saved record lists ${dateLabel} as the most recent period start.`,
+        `I can see a period start dated ${dateLabel} in your cycle log. If that doesn’t look right, you can update the check-in.`,
+        `The most recent period start I see in your saved entries is ${dateLabel}.`,
+      ])
+    }
+    return choose([
+      'I don’t see an explicitly saved period-start date in the cycle context available to me, so I can’t say when your last period began.',
+      'There isn’t a period-start date in the saved entries I can see. I won’t infer it from a phase label or a mood check-in.',
+      'I can’t find a recorded start date in the cycle summary available to me. If you log one, I can refer to that date later.',
+      'I don’t have a saved period-start date to check right now; I’d rather not guess.',
+    ])
+  }
+
+  if (/pain|cramp|bleed|spotting|period|symptom|pregnan|fertil|ovulat|pms|pmdd|menopaus|cycle/.test(query)) {
+    return choose([
+      'I can help with cycle and reproductive-health education, but I can’t diagnose or predict pregnancy from a check-in. If a symptom is severe, unusual, or a big change for you, contact a qualified clinician.',
+      'Cycle experiences vary, and tracking can’t confirm ovulation or pregnancy. Tell me which symptom or cycle question you mean, and I’ll give you the clearest general information I can.',
+      'I’m happy to unpack the science or practical options. I can’t diagnose symptoms, though; sudden or severe pain, very heavy bleeding, fainting, or fever with pelvic pain calls for prompt medical care.',
+      'That’s worth understanding. I can explain common cycle symptoms and what’s known, while being honest about uncertainty. What part would you like to focus on?',
+    ])
+  }
+
+  if (/phase|menstrual|follicular|ovulat|luteal/.test(query)) {
+    if (phaseLabel) {
+      return choose([
+        `Your most recently logged phase is ${phaseLabel}. Experiences differ widely, so it’s context—not a script for your mood, energy, or desire.`,
+        `The latest phase you selected was ${phaseLabel}. A phase label can help organize notes, but it can’t predict exactly how you’ll feel.`,
+        `I can see ${phaseLabel} as your most recent logged phase. Want the science of that phase, common experiences, or practical care ideas?`,
+        `Your latest check-in names the ${phaseLabel} phase. Bodies vary, and the phase alone doesn’t tell me what’s happening for you today.`,
+      ])
+    }
+    return choose([
+      'I don’t see a saved phase to personalize this yet. I can still explain the phases generally—what would you like to know?',
+      'I don’t have a recent phase logged, and I won’t guess. If you tell me what you’re curious about, I can explain the cycle in general terms.',
+      'There isn’t a recent phase in the saved context I can see. We can still talk through cycle science without personal data.',
+      'I can explain any phase, but I don’t have a saved one to refer to right now. Is there a specific phase or symptom on your mind?',
+    ])
+  }
+
+  return choose([
+    'I’m listening. I can help with cycle questions, everyday life, relationships, or just sorting through a thought—where would you like to begin?',
+    'That could go a few directions. What would help most: a clear answer, practical options, or space to think it through?',
+    'Tell me what part matters most to you, and I’ll meet you there rather than steering everything back to your cycle.',
+    'I’m here for the real question, not just the neat one. What would you like to untangle first?',
+    'We can take this at your pace. What are you hoping to understand or decide?',
+  ])
+}
+
+const chooseLocalVariant = (variants, conversation = []) => {
+  const recentReplies = conversation
+    .filter((item) => item?.role === 'assistant' && typeof item.content === 'string')
+    .slice(-3)
+    .map((item) => normalizeReply(item.content))
+  const unseen = variants.filter((variant) => !recentReplies.includes(normalizeReply(variant)))
+  const choices = unseen.length ? unseen : variants
+  return choices[Math.floor(Math.random() * choices.length)]
+}
+
+const normalizeReply = (value) => String(value).toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
+
+const isValidDateString = (value) => {
+  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) return false
+  const date = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00.000Z`)
+  return Number.isFinite(date.getTime()) && date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === value
+}
+
+const dateStringDistance = (start, end) => {
+  const startTime = new Date(`${start}T00:00:00.000Z`).getTime()
+  const endTime = new Date(`${end}T00:00:00.000Z`).getTime()
+  return Math.round((endTime - startTime) / 86400000)
+}
+
+const averageField = (records, field) => {
+  const values = records.map((record) => numericScale(record[field])).filter((value) => value !== null)
+  return values.length ? values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length : null
+}
+
+const numericScale = (value) => {
+  const number = Number(value)
+  return Number.isFinite(number) && number >= 1 && number <= 10 ? number : null
+}
+
+const localDateAtNoon = (date) => new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate(), 12)

+import { makeLocalReply, summarizeCycles } from './luna'
+
+describe('Luna context and local conversation', () => {
+  test('uses explicit unique period starts to summarize the last start and observed average', () => {
+    const summary = summarizeCycles([
+      { date: '2025-01-01', phase: 'menstrual', mood: 5, energy: 5, period_start_date: '2025-01-01' },
+      { date: '2025-01-02', phase: 'menstrual', mood: 5, energy: 4, period_start_date: '2025-01-01' },
+      { date: '2025-01-29', phase: 'follicular', mood: 6, energy: 6, period_start_date: '2025-01-29' },
+      { date: '2025-03-01', phase: 'menstrual', mood: 6, energy: 6, period_start_date: '2025-03-01' },
+      { date: '2025-03-04', phase: 'menstrual', mood: 6, energy: 6, period_start_date: '2025-02-31' },
+    ])
+
+    expect(summary.lastPeriodStartDate).toBe('2025-03-01')
+    expect(summary.averageCycleLength).toBe(29.5)
+    expect(summary.cycleLengthObservations).toBe(2)
+  })
+
+  test('varies local replies instead of repeating one of the recent answers', () => {
+    const conversation = []
+    const replies = []
+
+    for (let index = 0; index < 4; index += 1) {
+      const reply = makeLocalReply('hello', {}, null, 'balanced', conversation)
+      expect(conversation.slice(-3).some((item) => item.role === 'assistant' && item.content === reply)).toBe(false)
+      replies.push(reply)
+      conversation.push({ role: 'assistant', content: reply })
+    }
+
+    expect(new Set(replies).size).toBe(4)
+  })
+})
diff --git a/src/lib/period.js b/src/lib/period.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..53c0293a4cf75cafe4c900b2837a22cbd31e4ec9
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/lib/period.js
@@ -0,0 +1,32 @@
+export const localDateString = (date = new Date()) => {
+  const year = date.getFullYear()
+  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')
+  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')
+  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`
+}
+
+export const estimatePeriodEndDate = (startDate, duration) => {
+  const days = Number(duration)
+  const endDate = parseLocalDate(startDate)
+  if (!endDate || !Number.isInteger(days) || days < 1 || days > 10) return ''
+
+  endDate.setDate(endDate.getDate() + days - 1)
+  return localDateString(endDate)
+}
+
+export const formatPeriodDate = (dateString, options = {}) => {
+  const date = parseLocalDate(dateString)
+  if (!date) return ''
+  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
+    weekday: 'long',
+    month: 'long',
+    day: 'numeric',
+    ...options,
+  }).format(date)
+}
+
+const parseLocalDate = (dateString) => {
+  if (typeof dateString !== 'string' || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateString)) return null
+  const date = new Date(`${dateString}T12:00:00`)
+  return Number.isFinite(date.getTime()) && localDateString(date) === dateString ? date : null
+}
diff --git a/src/lib/period.test.js b/src/lib/period.test.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..d4deaad95af8603918d32129c4a0ef0ea1a7de8a

@@ -0,0 +1,26 @@
+import { estimatePeriodEndDate, formatPeriodDate, localDateString } from './period'
+
+describe('period date helpers', () => {
+  test('formats a date using the local calendar, not a UTC day shift', () => {
+    expect(localDateString(new Date(2026, 1, 28, 23, 45))).toBe('2026-02-28')
+  })
+
+  test('estimates the inclusive last day across month, leap-year, and year boundaries', () => {
+    expect(estimatePeriodEndDate('2026-02-27', 5)).toBe('2026-03-03')
+    expect(estimatePeriodEndDate('2024-02-27', 5)).toBe('2024-03-02')
+    expect(estimatePeriodEndDate('2025-12-31', 3)).toBe('2026-01-02')
+  })
+
+  test('accepts a one-day duration and rejects invalid dates or durations', () => {
+    expect(estimatePeriodEndDate('2026-05-18', 1)).toBe('2026-05-18')
+    expect(estimatePeriodEndDate('2026-02-31', 5)).toBe('')
+    expect(estimatePeriodEndDate('2026-05-18', 0)).toBe('')
+    expect(estimatePeriodEndDate('2026-05-18', 11)).toBe('')
+    expect(estimatePeriodEndDate('2026-05-18', 2.5)).toBe('')
+  })
+
+  test('formats valid calendar dates and leaves invalid dates empty', () => {
+    expect(formatPeriodDate('2026-03-03')).not.toBe('')
+    expect(formatPeriodDate('2026-02-31')).toBe('')
+  })
+})

+import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
+import { Alert, Button, TextField } from '@mui/material'
+import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
+import { GiLotus } from 'react-icons/gi'
+import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
+
+import { isSupabaseConfigured, supabase } from '../lib/supabase'
+
+const authDestination = () => {
+  try {
+    if (window.sessionStorage.getItem('hercycle.pendingCheckIn')) return '/log'
+    if (window.sessionStorage.getItem('hercycle.pendingIntimacy')) return '/intimacy'
+    return '/'
+  } catch {
+    return '/'
+  }
+}
+
+const Auth = ({ session }) => {
+  const { t } = useTranslation()
+  const navigate = useNavigate()
+  const [mode, setMode] = useState('signin')
+  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
+  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
+  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)
+  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState(null)
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (session) navigate(authDestination(), { replace: true })
+  }, [session, navigate])
+
+  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
+    event.preventDefault()
+    setFeedback(null)
+
+    if (!email.trim() || !/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(email)) {
+      setFeedback({ severity: 'error', text: t('invalidEmail') })
+      return
+    }
+    if (password.length < 8) {
+      setFeedback({ severity: 'error', text: t('passwordTooShort') })
+      return
+    }
+
+    setIsSubmitting(true)
+    try {
+      const result = mode === 'signin'
+        ? await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email: email.trim(), password })
+        : await supabase.auth.signUp({
+          email: email.trim(),
+          password,
+          options: { emailRedirectTo: window.location.origin },
+        })
+
+      if (result.error) throw result.error
+
+      if (result.data.session) {
+        try {
+          await seedFirstAccountPreferences(result.data.session.user.id)
+        } catch (preferenceError) {
+          // Authentication remains successful even if preference sync needs a retry.
+          console.warn('Could not sync initial preferences:', preferenceError)
+        }
+        navigate(authDestination(), { replace: true })
+      } else {
+        setFeedback({ severity: 'success', text: t('signUpSuccess') })
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      setFeedback({ severity: 'error', text: error.message || t('errorGeneric') })
+    } finally {
+      setIsSubmitting(false)
+    }
+  }
+
+  const isSignIn = mode === 'signin'
+
+  if (session) {
+    return (
+      <main className="auth-page">
+        <section className="auth-card" role="status">Returning you to your private space…</section>
+      </main>
+    )
+  }
+
+  if (!isSupabaseConfigured || !supabase) {
+    return (
+      <main className="auth-page">
+        <div className="auth-background-bloom auth-background-bloom-one" />
+        <section className="auth-card auth-setup-card">
+          <div className="auth-card-icon"><GiLotus aria-hidden="true" /></div>
+          <span className="auth-eyebrow">YOUR SPACE, YOUR CHOICE</span>
+          <h1>Explore as a guest.</h1>
+          <p className="auth-description">
+            You can use HerCycle without an account. Cycle check-ins and intimate reflections are only saved when you sign in.
+          </p>
+          <Link to="/" className="auth-return-button">Continue without signing in</Link>
+        </section>
+        <p className="auth-footnote">Use a private device for cycle and intimate health information.</p>
+      </main>
+    )
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <main className="auth-page">
+      <div className="auth-background-bloom auth-background-bloom-one" />
+      <div className="auth-background-bloom auth-background-bloom-two" />
+      <Link className="auth-brand" to="/onboarding">
+        <span className="app-brand-mark"><GiLotus aria-hidden="true" /></span>
+        <span>hercycle</span>
+      </Link>
+
+      <section className="auth-card">
+        <div className="auth-card-icon"><GiLotus aria-hidden="true" /></div>
+        <span className="auth-eyebrow">A SPACE THAT IS YOURS</span>
+        <h1>{isSignIn ? t('welcomeBack') : t('createYourAccount')}</h1>
+        <p className="auth-description">{t('authHelp')}</p>
+
+        {feedback && <Alert severity={feedback.severity} className="auth-alert">{feedback.text}</Alert>}
+
+        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="auth-form">
+          <TextField
+            fullWidth
+            label={t('email')}
+            type="email"
+            autoComplete="email"
+            value={email}
+            onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
+            required
+          />
+          <TextField
+            fullWidth
+            label={t('password')}
+            type="password"
+            autoComplete={isSignIn ? 'current-password' : 'new-password'}
+            value={password}
+            onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
+            required
+            inputProps={{ minLength: 8 }}
+          />
+          <Button
+            fullWidth
+            variant="contained"
+            type="submit"
+            disabled={isSubmitting}
+            className="onboarding-cta auth-submit"
+          >
+            {isSubmitting ? 'One moment…' : isSignIn ? t('signIn') : t('signUp')}
+          </Button>
+        </form>
+
+        <div className="auth-mode-switch">
+          <span>{isSignIn ? "New to HerCycle?" : 'Already have an account?'}</span>
+          <button type="button" onClick={() => { setMode(isSignIn ? 'signup' : 'signin'); setFeedback(null) }}>
+            {isSignIn ? t('signUp') : t('signIn')}
+          </button>
+        </div>
+        <Link to="/onboarding" className="auth-back-link">Back to welcome</Link>
+      </section>
+
+      <p className="auth-footnote">Your private health information is never used for advertising.</p>
+    </main>
+  )
+}
+
+const seedFirstAccountPreferences = async (userId) => {
+  let onboarding = {}
+  let preferences = {}
+  try {
+    onboarding = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('hercycle.onboarding') || '{}')
+    preferences = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('hercycle.preferences') || '{}')
+  } catch {
+    return
+  }
+
+  const { data: existing, error: loadError } = await supabase
+    .from('user_settings')
+    .select('user_id')
+    .eq('user_id', userId)
+    .maybeSingle()
+  if (loadError) throw loadError
+  if (existing || !onboarding.phase_selected) return
+
+  const { error } = await supabase.from('user_settings').upsert({
+    user_id: userId,
+    onboarding_completed: true,
+    phase_selected: onboarding.phase_selected,
+    tone_preference: onboarding.tone_preference || 'balanced',
+    theme_preference: preferences.theme || 'light',
+    font_size: preferences.fontSize || 'medium',
+    hide_intimacy: Boolean(preferences.hideIntimacy),
+    updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+  }, { onConflict: 'user_id' })
+  if (error) throw error
+}
+
+export default Auth

+const ResponsiveChart = ({ moodPoints, energyPoints, cycles }) => (
+  <div className="chart-wrap">
+    <svg className="insight-chart" viewBox="0 0 700 190" role="img" aria-label="Mood and energy ratings across recent check-ins">
+      {[1, 3, 5, 7, 10].map((line) => {
+        const y = 158 - ((line - 1) / 9) * 131
+        return <g key={line}><line x1="36" x2="686" y1={y} y2={y} className="chart-grid-line" /><text x="12" y={y + 4} className="chart-axis-label">{line}</text></g>
+      })}
+      {moodPoints && <polyline points={moodPoints} className="chart-line chart-line-mood" />}
+      {energyPoints && <polyline points={energyPoints} className="chart-line chart-line-energy" />}
+      {cycles.map((cycle, index) => {
+        const x = 44 + (index * 630) / Math.max(cycles.length - 1, 1)
+        const mood = Number(cycle.mood)
+        const y = mood > 0 ? 158 - ((Math.min(mood, 10) - 1) / 9) * 131 : null
+        return y === null ? null : <circle key={cycle.id} cx={x} cy={y} r="4.5" className="chart-point" />
+      })}
+    </svg>
+    <div className="chart-axis-dates">
+      <span>{cycles[0]?.date}</span>
+      <span>{cycles[cycles.length - 1]?.date}</span>
+    </div>
+    <div className="chart-legend-row"><span><i className="legend-dot legend-dot-mood" />Mood</span><span><i className="legend-dot legend-dot-energy" />Energy</span></div>
+  </div>
+)
+
+const average = (records, key) => {
+  const values = records.map((record) => Number(record[key])).filter((value) => Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0)
+  if (!values.length) return null
+  return values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length
+}
+
+const makePoints = (records, key) => {
+  const available = records.map((record, index) => ({ index, value: Number(record[key]) })).filter((item) => Number.isFinite(item.value) && item.value > 0)
+  if (available.length < 2) return ''
+  const maxIndex = Math.max(records.length - 1, 1)
+  return available.map(({ index, value }) => {
+    const x = 44 + (index * 630) / maxIndex
+    const y = 158 - ((Math.min(Math.max(value, 1), 10) - 1) / 9) * 131
+    return `${x},${y}`
+  }).join(' ')
+}
