+import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
+import { Alert, Skeleton } from '@mui/material'
+import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion'
+import {
+  FiArrowDown,
+  FiHeart,
+  FiLock,
+  FiSend,
+  FiShield,
+  FiStar,
+  FiTrash2,
+} from 'react-icons/fi'
+import { GiLotus } from 'react-icons/gi'
+import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next'
+
+import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase'
+import {
+  MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH,
+  makeLocalReply,
+  readAiPreference,
+  readOnboardingPhase,
+  readOnboardingTone,
+  requestLunaReply,
+  summarizeCycles,
+  writeAiPreference,
+} from '../lib/luna'
+
+const LunaChat = ({ session }) => {
+  const { t } = useTranslation()
+  const [cycles, setCycles] = useState([])
+  const [isLoadingCycles, setIsLoadingCycles] = useState(Boolean(session?.user?.id))
+  const [contextError, setContextError] = useState('')
+  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
+  const [isThinking, setIsThinking] = useState(false)
+  const [aiPreference, setAiPreference] = useState(() => readAiPreference(session?.user?.id))
+  const aiConsent = aiPreference === 'yes'
+  const [connectionMode, setConnectionMode] = useState(null)
+  const [chatError, setChatError] = useState('')
+  const [messages, setMessages] = useState(() => [{
+    id: 'luna-welcome',
+    role: 'assistant',
+    content: t('lunaHello'),
+  }])
+  const messagesEndRef = useRef(null)
+  const inputRef = useRef(null)
+  const activeAiRequestRef = useRef(null)
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let isMounted = true
+    const loadCycleContext = async () => {
+      if (!session?.user?.id) {
+        setCycles([])
+        setIsLoadingCycles(false)
+        return
+      }
+
+      setIsLoadingCycles(true)
+      setContextError('')
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('cycles')
+        .select('date, phase, mood, energy, period_start_date')
+        .eq('user_id', session.user.id)
+        .order('date', { ascending: false })
+        .limit(60)
+
+      if (!isMounted) return
+      if (error) {
+        console.error('Luna could not load cycle context:', error)
+        setContextError(t('lunaContextLoadError'))
+      } else {
+        setCycles(data || [])
+      }
+      setIsLoadingCycles(false)
+    }
+
+    loadCycleContext().catch((error) => {
+      if (isMounted) {
+        console.error('Luna could not load cycle context:', error)
+        setContextError(t('lunaContextLoadError'))
+        setIsLoadingCycles(false)
+      }
+    })
+
+    return () => { isMounted = false }
+  }, [session?.user?.id, t])
+
+  useEffect(() => () => activeAiRequestRef.current?.abort(), [])
+
+  const cycleSummary = useMemo(() => summarizeCycles(cycles), [cycles])
+  const localPhase = readOnboardingPhase()
+  const localTone = readOnboardingTone()
+  const isConsentRequired = Boolean(session?.user?.id)
+  const consentIsPending = isConsentRequired && aiPreference === 'unset'
+  const canChat = !isThinking
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    const node = messagesEndRef.current
+    if (!node) return
+    const reduceMotion = Boolean(window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches)
+    node.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduceMotion ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'end' })
+  }, [messages, isThinking])
+
+  const setAiPreferenceForSession = (preference) => {
+    writeAiPreference(session?.user?.id, preference)
+    setAiPreference(preference)
+  }
+
+  const allowAiChat = () => setAiPreferenceForSession('yes')
+  const continueLocally = () => setAiPreferenceForSession('no')
+  const turnOffAiChat = () => {
+    activeAiRequestRef.current?.abort()
+    setAiPreferenceForSession('no')
+    setConnectionMode('local')
+    setMessages([{
+      id: `luna-welcome-${Date.now()}`,
+      role: 'assistant',
+      content: t('lunaHello'),
+    }])
+    setChatError('')
+  }
+
+  const clearConversation = () => {
+    activeAiRequestRef.current?.abort()
+    setMessage('')
+    setMessages([{
+      id: `luna-welcome-${Date.now()}`,
+      role: 'assistant',
+      content: t('lunaHello'),
+    }])
+    setChatError('')
+    inputRef.current?.focus()
+  }
+
+  const sendMessage = async (suggestedMessage) => {
+    const text = String(suggestedMessage ?? message).trim().slice(0, MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH)
+    if (!text || !canChat) return
+
+    const userMessage = { id: `user-${Date.now()}`, role: 'user', content: text }
+    const nextMessages = [...messages, userMessage]
+    setMessages(nextMessages)
+    setMessage('')
+    setChatError('')
+    setIsThinking(true)
+
+    let requestController = null
+    try {
+      let aiReply = null
+      if (session?.access_token && aiConsent) {
+        requestController = new AbortController()
+        activeAiRequestRef.current = requestController
+        aiReply = await requestLunaReply(session.access_token, nextMessages, cycleSummary, requestController.signal)
+      }
+
+      if (aiReply) {
+        setConnectionMode('ai')
+        setChatError('')
+        setMessages((current) => [...current, {
+          id: `luna-${Date.now()}`,
+          role: 'assistant',
+          content: aiReply,
+        }])
+      } else {
+        setConnectionMode('local')
+        if (session?.access_token && aiConsent) setChatError(t('lunaAiUnavailable'))
+        setMessages((current) => [...current, {
+          id: `luna-local-${Date.now()}`,
+          role: 'assistant',
+          content: makeLocalReply(text, cycleSummary, localPhase, localTone, nextMessages),
+        }])
+      }
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (error?.name === 'AbortError') return
+      console.info('Luna AI endpoint unavailable; using local companion replies.', error)
+      setConnectionMode('local')
+      if (session?.access_token && aiConsent) setChatError(t('lunaAiUnavailable'))
+      setMessages((current) => [...current, {
+        id: `luna-local-${Date.now()}`,
+        role: 'assistant',
+        content: makeLocalReply(text, cycleSummary, localPhase, localTone, nextMessages),
+      }])
+    } finally {
+      if (activeAiRequestRef.current === requestController) activeAiRequestRef.current = null
+      setIsThinking(false)
+    }
+  }
+
+  const handleSubmit = (event) => {
+    event.preventDefault()
+    sendMessage()
+  }
+
+  const handleKeyDown = (event) => {
+    if (event.key === 'Enter' && !event.shiftKey) {
+      event.preventDefault()
+      sendMessage()
+    }
+  }
+
+  const suggestions = [
+    t('lunaPromptPatterns'),
+    t('lunaPromptEnergy'),
+    t('lunaPromptPhase'),
+    t('lunaPromptIntimacy'),
+    t('lunaPromptGeneral'),
+    t('lunaPromptDoctor'),
+  ]
+
+  return (
+    <div className="luna-page">
+      <div className="luna-layout">
+        <motion.section
+          className="luna-chat-card"
+          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 14 }}
+          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
+          transition={{ duration: 0.32, ease: 'easeOut' }}
+          aria-label={t('lunaChatLabel')}
+        >
+          <header className="luna-chat-header">
+            <div className="luna-chat-brand">
+              <div className="luna-avatar luna-avatar-header" aria-hidden="true">
+                <span className="luna-avatar-glow" />
+                <GiLotus />
+                <span className="luna-avatar-spark">✦</span>
+              </div>
+              <div>
+                <div className="luna-name-row"><h2>Luna</h2><span className={`luna-status-dot${connectionMode === 'ai' ? ' is-ai' : ''}`} /></div>
+                <p>{connectionMode === 'ai' ? t('lunaModeAi') : connectionMode === 'local' ? t('lunaModeLocal') : t('lunaModeReady')}</p>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+            <button type="button" className="luna-clear-button" onClick={clearConversation} aria-label={t('lunaClearChat')} title={t('lunaClearChat')}>
+              <FiTrash2 aria-hidden="true" /><span>{t('lunaClearShort')}</span>
+            </button>
+          </header>
+
+          {contextError && <Alert severity="info" className="luna-inline-alert">{contextError}</Alert>}
+
+          {consentIsPending && (
+            <section className="luna-consent-banner" aria-label={t('lunaPrivacyHeading')}>
+              <span className="luna-consent-icon"><FiShield aria-hidden="true" /></span>
+              <div className="luna-consent-banner-copy">
+                <span className="eyebrow-label">{t('lunaConsentEyebrow')}</span>
+                <strong>{t('lunaPrivacyHeading')}</strong>
+                <p>{t('lunaPrivacyConsent')}</p>
+              </div>
+              <div className="luna-consent-actions">
+                <button type="button" className="luna-ai-enable" onClick={allowAiChat}>{t('lunaConsentButton')} <FiHeart aria-hidden="true" /></button>
+                <button type="button" className="luna-local-only" onClick={continueLocally}>{t('lunaPreviewButton')}</button>
+              </div>
+            </section>
+          )}
+          <>
+              <div className="luna-message-list" role="log" aria-live="polite" aria-relevant="additions text">
+                {messages.map((item, index) => (
+                  <motion.article
+                    key={item.id}
+                    className={`luna-message luna-message-${item.role}`}
+                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8, scale: 0.985 }}
+                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
+                    transition={{ duration: 0.22, delay: index === messages.length - 1 ? 0.03 : 0 }}
+                  >
+                    {item.role === 'assistant' && (
+                      <span className="luna-message-avatar" aria-hidden="true"><GiLotus /></span>
+                    )}
+                    <div className="luna-bubble-wrap">
+                      {item.role === 'assistant' && <span className="luna-message-speaker">Luna</span>}
+                      <p className="luna-bubble">{item.content}</p>
+                    </div>
+                  </motion.article>
+                ))}
+                <AnimatePresence>
+                  {isThinking && (
+                    <motion.div className="luna-message luna-message-assistant" initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 6 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} exit={{ opacity: 0 }}>
+                      <span className="luna-message-avatar" aria-hidden="true"><GiLotus /></span>
+                      <div className="luna-bubble-wrap">
+                        <span className="luna-message-speaker">Luna</span>
+                        <div className="luna-typing-bubble" role="status" aria-label={t('lunaThinking')}>
+                          <span /><span /><span />
+                        </div>
+                      </div>
+                    </motion.div>
+                  )}
+                </AnimatePresence>
+                <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
+              </div>
+
+              {messages.length === 1 && !isThinking && (
+                <div className="luna-suggestions">
+                  <span className="luna-suggestions-label"><FiStar aria-hidden="true" /> {t('lunaStarters')}</span>
+                  <div className="luna-suggestion-list">
+                    {suggestions.map((suggestion, index) => (
+                      <motion.button
+                        key={suggestion}
+                        type="button"
+                        className={`luna-suggestion luna-suggestion-${index + 1}`}
+                        onClick={() => sendMessage(suggestion)}
+                        whileHover={{ y: -2 }}
+                        whileTap={{ scale: 0.98 }}
+                        disabled={!canChat}
+                      >
+                        {suggestion}<FiArrowDown aria-hidden="true" />
+                      </motion.button>
+                    ))}
+                  </div>
+                </div>
+              )}
+
+              {chatError && <Alert severity="error" className="luna-inline-alert">{chatError}</Alert>}
+
+              <form className="luna-composer" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
+                <label className="visually-hidden" htmlFor="luna-message">{t('lunaInputPlaceholder')}</label>
+                <textarea
+                  id="luna-message"
+                  ref={inputRef}
+                  rows={1}
+                  maxLength={MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH}
+                  placeholder={t('lunaInputPlaceholder')}
+                  value={message}
+                  disabled={!canChat}
+                  onChange={(event) => setMessage(event.target.value)}
+                  onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
+                />
+                <span className="luna-compose-hint">{t('lunaComposeHint')}</span>
+                <button type="submit" className="luna-send-button" disabled={!message.trim() || !canChat} aria-label={t('lunaSend')}>
+                  <FiSend aria-hidden="true" />
+                </button>
+              </form>

+-- Align an existing HerCycle Supabase project with the current frontend.
+-- Safe to apply more than once. For a brand-new project, run schema.sql instead.
+
+-- New profile and check-in fields used by onboarding, settings, and dashboards.
+alter table public.user_settings
+  add column if not exists phase_selected text not null default 'menstrual',
+  add column if not exists hide_intimacy boolean not null default false;
+
+alter table public.cycles
+  add column if not exists energy integer,
+  add column if not exists period_start_date date,
+  add column if not exists period_duration_days smallint;
+
+do $$
+begin
+  if not exists (
+    select 1 from pg_constraint
+    where conrelid = 'public.cycles'::regclass
+      and conname = 'cycles_period_duration_days_range'
+  ) then
+    alter table public.cycles
+      add constraint cycles_period_duration_days_range
+      check (period_duration_days is null or period_duration_days between 1 and 10);
+  end if;
+end
+$$;
+
+alter table public.intimacy_logs
+  add column if not exists desire_vibe text not null default 'tender';
+
+do $$
+begin
+  if not exists (
+    select 1 from pg_constraint
+    where conrelid = 'public.intimacy_logs'::regclass
+      and conname = 'intimacy_logs_desire_vibe_allowed'
+  ) then
+    alter table public.intimacy_logs
+      add constraint intimacy_logs_desire_vibe_allowed
+      check (desire_vibe in ('tender', 'playful', 'naughty'));
+  end if;
+end
+$$;
+
+-- Keep all personal tables owner-scoped. In particular, do not leave old
+-- permissive service-role policies or grants to anon/public on the tables.
+alter table public.user_settings enable row level security;
+alter table public.cycles enable row level security;
+alter table public.intimacy_logs enable row level security;
+alter table public.quests enable row level security;
+alter table public.user_progress enable row level security;
+
+drop policy if exists "Users can read own settings" on public.user_settings;
+drop policy if exists "Users can insert own settings" on public.user_settings;
+drop policy if exists "Users can update own settings" on public.user_settings;
+drop policy if exists "Service role can all operations" on public.user_settings;
+create policy "Users can read own settings"
+  on public.user_settings for select to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can insert own settings"
+  on public.user_settings for insert to authenticated
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can update own settings"
+  on public.user_settings for update to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id)
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+
+drop policy if exists "Users can read own cycles" on public.cycles;
+drop policy if exists "Users can insert own cycles" on public.cycles;
+drop policy if exists "Users can update own cycles" on public.cycles;
+drop policy if exists "Users can delete own cycles" on public.cycles;
+drop policy if exists "Service role can all operations" on public.cycles;
+create policy "Users can read own cycles"
+  on public.cycles for select to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can insert own cycles"
+  on public.cycles for insert to authenticated
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can update own cycles"
+  on public.cycles for update to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id)
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can delete own cycles"
+  on public.cycles for delete to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+
+drop policy if exists "Users can read own intimacy logs" on public.intimacy_logs;
+drop policy if exists "Users can insert own intimacy logs" on public.intimacy_logs;
+drop policy if exists "Users can update own intimacy logs" on public.intimacy_logs;
+drop policy if exists "Users can delete own intimacy logs" on public.intimacy_logs;
+drop policy if exists "Service role can all operations" on public.intimacy_logs;
+create policy "Users can read own intimacy logs"
+  on public.intimacy_logs for select to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can insert own intimacy logs"
+  on public.intimacy_logs for insert to authenticated
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can update own intimacy logs"
+  on public.intimacy_logs for update to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id)
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can delete own intimacy logs"
+  on public.intimacy_logs for delete to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+
+drop policy if exists "Users can read own quests" on public.quests;
+drop policy if exists "Users can insert own quests" on public.quests;
+drop policy if exists "Users can update own quests" on public.quests;
+drop policy if exists "Users can delete own quests" on public.quests;
+drop policy if exists "Service role can all operations" on public.quests;
+create policy "Users can read own quests"
+  on public.quests for select to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can insert own quests"
+  on public.quests for insert to authenticated
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can update own quests"
+  on public.quests for update to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id)
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can delete own quests"
+  on public.quests for delete to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+
+drop policy if exists "Users can read own progress" on public.user_progress;
+drop policy if exists "Users can insert own progress" on public.user_progress;
+drop policy if exists "Users can update own progress" on public.user_progress;
+drop policy if exists "Users can delete own progress" on public.user_progress;
+drop policy if exists "Service role can all operations" on public.user_progress;
+create policy "Users can read own progress"
+  on public.user_progress for select to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can insert own progress"
+  on public.user_progress for insert to authenticated
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can update own progress"
+  on public.user_progress for update to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id)
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+create policy "Users can delete own progress"
+  on public.user_progress for delete to authenticated
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+
+revoke all on public.user_settings, public.cycles, public.intimacy_logs, public.quests, public.user_progress
+  from public, anon, authenticated;
+grant select, insert, update, delete on public.user_settings, public.cycles, public.intimacy_logs, public.quests, public.user_progress
+  to authenticated;
+
+create index if not exists idx_cycles_user_id on public.cycles (user_id);
+create index if not exists idx_cycles_date on public.cycles (date);
+create index if not exists idx_intimacy_user_id on public.intimacy_logs (user_id);
+create index if not exists idx_intimacy_date on public.intimacy_logs (date);
+create index if not exists idx_quests_user_id on public.quests (user_id);
+
+-- Update the older RPC signature so period metadata is accepted consistently.
+drop function if exists public.insert_cycle_log(uuid, text, integer, integer, integer, boolean, date);
+create or replace function public.insert_cycle_log(
+  p_user_id uuid,
+  p_phase text,
+  p_mood integer,
+  p_libido integer,
+  p_intimacy_level integer,
+  p_period boolean,
+  p_date date,
+  p_period_start_date date default null,
+  p_period_duration_days smallint default null
+)
+returns jsonb
+language plpgsql
+as $$
+declare
+  v_result jsonb;
+begin
+  insert into public.cycles as cycle (
+    user_id, phase, mood, libido, intimacy_level, period, date,
+    period_start_date, period_duration_days
+  )
+  values (
+    p_user_id, p_phase, p_mood, p_libido, p_intimacy_level, p_period, p_date,
+    p_period_start_date, p_period_duration_days
+  )
+  returning to_jsonb(cycle) into v_result;
+
+  update public.user_progress
+  set total_cycles = total_cycles + 1,
+      current_streak = case when current_streak > 0 then current_streak + 1 else 1 end,
+      updated_at = now()
+  where user_id = p_user_id;
+
+  return v_result;
+end
+$$;
